HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3374a763a4edfe8a6096","text":"1. **Social withdrawal concentrates grief into the seeing organ.** و ل ي B007 (turning away), ء س ف B001 (grief over loss), ب ي ض B001, ع ي ن B001, and ح ز ن B001 form an affective-bodily mechanism. I infer that prolonged rough sorrow registers materially as whitened eyes. The ayah reads grief as embodied alteration, not speech alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_66d629bfae641f1bcbc2","text":"Later 12:93-96 reversed the embodied grief through the shirt, restoring the same eyes in which sorrow had become visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001303/B001","root_001303/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_934b68afadbec00d1c20","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"12:84:12\",\"root_001303\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Acıyı içinde tutmak duyguyu yok etmez; onun kime ve nasıl açılacağını denetleyip sonraki arama emrine kadar eylem gücünü korur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001303/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_98d2ae6aaf48d032a17e","text":"2. **Contained feeling opposes outward diffusion.** ك ظ م B001/B002 (holding anger/breath inside) follows the vocal lament. I infer that only a compressed portion reaches speech while the larger affect remains held. Expression and containment coexist.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001303/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c09eb29807f5b85e7257","text":"1. **Grief held inward without extinguishing directed action.** `ك ظ م:B001` is holding affect inside rather than displaying it, and `ك ظ م:B002` is breath held back in silence or distress. Jacob turns away, names his grief, and bears its bodily effect; yet 12:86 directs complaint to God and 12:87 orders an active search. The packet supplies containment, selective expression, and later action; I infer that *kaẓīm* marks regulation of audience and outlet, not absence of feeling or agency. The ayah reads as compressed grief that still feeds purposeful movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc62906b0290ed7271a8","text":"Later 12:86 reveals where the contained grief goes: Jacob directs complaint and sorrow to God, showing that restraint preserves selective, purposeful disclosure rather than suppressing expression altogether.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000032/B001","root_000168/B001","root_000317/B001","root_001069/B001","root_001684/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cfc44d53015f17d74c01","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"12:84:1\",\"root_001684\",[\"B007\"]],[\"12:84:4\",\"root_000032\",[\"B001\"]],[\"12:84:7\",\"root_000168\",[\"B001\"]],[\"12:84:8\",\"root_001069\",[\"B001\"]],[\"12:84:10\",\"root_000317\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Uzun süren keder, görme organında bedensel değişime dönüşür; böylece üzüntü yalnız söylenen değil, bedende taşınan bir iz olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
